Status: Completed

```
83791	## User
83792	
83793	请把当前项目改造成“实时语音转文本（STT）”模式，不要做翻译，不要做整段录音后上传，而是做真正的流式转写。
83794	
83795	目标要求：
83796	1. 持续监听麦克风，用户开始说话后立即采集音频。
83797	2. 不要使用“录制 N 秒 -> 停止 -> 上传 -> 再录制”的轮询式方案。
83798	3. 改为“持续录音 + 小块流式发送”：
83799	   - 将音频切成很小的 chunk，默认 100ms 一块
83800	   - 允许配置为 50ms / 100ms / 150ms
83801	4. 与语音转文字服务保持一条持续连接（优先 WebSocket；如果当前 SDK/服务更适合其他流式连接，也可以用等价流式方案）。
83802	5. 每收到 partial / interim transcription，就立刻更新前端字幕。

81	  storageKey: string,
82	  fallbackValue: number,
83	  minValue: number,
84	  maxValue: number,
85	): number {
86	  const storedValue = window.localStorage.getItem(storageKey);
87	  const parsedValue = storedValue ? Number(storedValue) : Number.NaN;
88	
89	  if (!Number.isFinite(parsedValue)) {
90	    return fallbackValue;
91	  }
92	
93	  return clamp(parsedValue, minValue, maxValue);
94	}

However, looking at what Deepgram provides - Deepgram does support WebSocket-based streaming transcription. But the current architecture goes through:
1. Electron main process spawns Python `lite_synphonia` which records audio using `sounddevice`
2. Python sends audio to Deepgram REST API
3. Results come back and are written to files


4. Electron reads the files

For true streaming STT, I'd need to rethink this entirely - the frontend would capture audio via Web Audio API and send chunks through IPC to the main process, which maintains a WebSocket connection to Deepgram's streaming API and streams results back. But the current Python backend does more than just transcription - it also handles summarization and PDF matching, so I need to figure out how to preserve that functionality while moving to streaming.

Looking at what's already been set up, the types are defined but the actual IPC handlers and frontend audio capture haven't been implemented. I should focus on just making the audio capture and transcription streaming while keeping the rest of the pipeline intact. Let me map out what needs to be added: IPC methods in the preload for starting and stopping realtime transcription plus receiving events, the TypeScript interface definitions, and the main process handlers that open the WebSocket connection to Deepgram and manage the streaming flow.

For the frontend, I need to set up microphone capture with an AudioWorklet, convert the audio to PCM16, add some basic silence detection, and handle the IPC communication with the main process. The key insight is that Deepgram's WebSocket streaming should run in the Electron main process since that's where Node.js packages like `ws` are available, so I need to check what dependencies are already in the package.json.
</thinking>

  saveLiteSynphoniaProviderSettings(payload) {
    return ipcRenderer.invoke("settings:save-lite-synphonia-provider-settings", payload);
  },

  startRealtimeTranscription(payload) {
    return ipcRenderer.invoke("workspace:start-realtime-transcription", payload);
  },
  pushRealtimeAudioChunk(payload) {
    return ipcRenderer.invoke("workspace:push-realtime-audio-chunk", payload);
  },
  stopRealtimeTranscription(workspaceId) {
    return ipcRenderer.invoke("workspace:stop-realtime-transcription", workspaceId);
  },
  onRealtimeTranscriptionEvent(callback) {
    const handler = (_event, data) => callback(data);
    ipcRenderer.on("realtime-transcription-event", handler);
    return () => {
      ipcRenderer.removeListener("realtime-transcription-event", handler);
    };
  },
});

  };

// ── Realtime streaming STT via Deepgram WebSocket ──────────────────
// Architecture: renderer captures mic audio via AudioWorklet, sends
// PCM16 chunks through IPC. Main process maintains a single WebSocket
// per workspace to Deepgram's streaming endpoint. Partial/final results
// are relayed back to renderer via webContents.send().

const realtimeSessions = new Map();

function emitRealtimeEvent(workspaceId, event) {
  for (const win of BrowserWindow.getAllWindows()) {
    win.webContents.send("realtime-transcription-event", {
      workspaceId,
      ...event,
    });
  }
}

async function getDeepgramApiKey() {
  const settings = await getLiteSynphoniaProviderSettingsSnapshot();
  return settings.deepgramApiKey;
}

function buildDeepgramStreamingUrl(sampleRate) {
  const params = new URLSearchParams({
    model: "nova-2",
    language: "zh",
    smart_format: "true",
    interim_results: "true",
    utterance_end_ms: "1500",
    vad_events: "true",
    encoding: "linear16",
    sample_rate: String(sampleRate),
    channels: "1",
  });
  return `wss://api.deepgram.com/v1/listen?${params.toString()}`;
}

function createRealtimeSession(workspaceId, chunkDurationMs, sampleRate) {
  return {
    workspaceId,
    chunkDurationMs,
    sampleRate,
    ws: null,
    partialText: "",
    finalTranscriptText: "",
    finalSegments: [],
    reconnectAttempts: 0,
    maxReconnectAttempts: 5,
    shouldReconnect: true,
    pendingChunks: [],
  };
}

async function connectDeepgramWebSocket(session) {
  const apiKey = await getDeepgramApiKey();
  if (!apiKey) {
    emitRealtimeEvent(session.workspaceId, {
      type: "error",
      status: "error",
      lastError: "Deepgram API Key 未配置，请在设置中填写。",
    });
    return;
  }

  emitRealtimeEvent(session.workspaceId, {
    type: "status",
    status: "connecting",
    message: "正在连接 Deepgram 流式转写服务...",
  });

  const url = buildDeepgramStreamingUrl(session.sampleRate);
  const ws = new WebSocket(url, {
    headers: { Authorization: `Token ${apiKey}` },
  });

  session.ws = ws;

  ws.on("open", () => {
    session.reconnectAttempts = 0;
    emitRealtimeEvent(session.workspaceId, {
      type: "status",
      status: "listening",
      message: "已连接，等待语音输入...",
    });
    for (const chunk of session.pendingChunks) {
      if (ws.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
        ws.send(chunk);
      }
    }
    session.pendingChunks = [];
  });

  ws.on("message", (data) => {
    try {
      const msg = JSON.parse(data.toString());

      if (msg.type === "Results") {
        const transcript = msg.channel?.alternatives?.[0]?.transcript || "";
        const isFinal = msg.is_final === true;

        if (!isFinal) {
          session.partialText = transcript;
          emitRealtimeEvent(session.workspaceId, {
            type: "partial",
            status: "streaming",
            partialText: transcript,
          });
        } else if (transcript.trim()) {
          const segment = {
            id: randomUUID(),
            text: transcript.trim(),
            finalizedAtUtc: new Date().toISOString(),
          };
          session.finalSegments.push(segment);
          session.finalTranscriptText +=
            (session.finalTranscriptText ? "\n" : "") + segment.text;
          session.partialText = "";

          emitRealtimeEvent(session.workspaceId, {
            type: "final",
            status: "streaming",
            finalSegment: segment,
            finalTranscriptText: session.finalTranscriptText,
            partialText: "",
          });

          const cacheRootPath = getWorkspaceCacheRoot();
          getIndexedWorkspace(cacheRootPath, session.workspaceId)
            .then(({ workspace }) => {
              if (workspace) {
                void appendTranscriptToSidebar(workspace, segment);
              }
            })
            .catch(() => {});
        }
      }

      if (msg.type === "UtteranceEnd") {
        if (session.partialText.trim()) {
          const segment = {
            id: randomUUID(),
            text: session.partialText.trim(),
            finalizedAtUtc: new Date().toISOString(),
          };
          session.finalSegments.push(segment);
          session.finalTranscriptText +=
            (session.finalTranscriptText ? "\n" : "") + segment.text;
          session.partialText = "";
          emitRealtimeEvent(session.workspaceId, {
            type: "final",
            status: "streaming",
            finalSegment: segment,
            finalTranscriptText: session.finalTranscriptText,
            partialText: "",
          });
        }
      }
    } catch (_err) {
      // ignore malformed messages
    }
  });

  ws.on("close", (code) => {
    session.ws = null;
    if (session.shouldReconnect && session.reconnectAttempts < session.maxReconnectAttempts) {
      session.reconnectAttempts++;
      const delay = Math.min(1000 * Math.pow(2, session.reconnectAttempts - 1), 8000);
      emitRealtimeEvent(session.workspaceId, {
        type: "status",
        status: "connecting",
        message: `连接断开 (${code})，${(delay / 1000).toFixed(0)}s 后重连 (${session.reconnectAttempts}/${session.maxReconnectAttempts})...`,
      });
      setTimeout(() => {
        if (session.shouldReconnect) {
          void connectDeepgramWebSocket(session);
        }
      }, delay);
    } else if (session.shouldReconnect) {
      emitRealtimeEvent(session.workspaceId, {
        type: "error",
        status: "error",
        lastError: `WebSocket 连接失败，已达最大重连次数 (${session.maxReconnectAttempts})。`,
      });
    } else {
      emitRealtimeEvent(session.workspaceId, {
        type: "stopped",
        status: "idle",
        message: "实时转写已停止。",
      });
    }
  });

  ws.on("error", (err) => {
    emitRealtimeEvent(session.workspaceId, {
      type: "error",
      status: "error",
      lastError: `WebSocket 错误: ${err.message}`,
    });
  });
}

async function appendTranscriptToSidebar(workspace, segment) {
  try {
    const sidebarPath = workspace.artifacts?.sidebarStatePath;
    if (!sidebarPath) return;

    let sidebarState;
    try {
      sidebarState = JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(sidebarPath, "utf8"));
    } catch {
      sidebarState = normalizeSidebarState(null);
    }

    if (!Array.isArray(sidebarState.finalSegments)) {
      sidebarState.finalSegments = [];
    }
    sidebarState.finalSegments.push(segment);
    sidebarState.finalTranscriptText =
      (sidebarState.finalTranscriptText || "") +
      (sidebarState.finalTranscriptText ? "\n" : "") +
      segment.text;
    sidebarState.updatedAtUtc = new Date().toISOString();
    sidebarState.schemaVersion = "2.0";

    const pendingTranscript =
      (sidebarState.summaryWindow?.pendingTranscript || "") + segment.text;
    sidebarState.summaryWindow = {
      ...sidebarState.summaryWindow,
      pendingTranscript,
      pendingChars: countVisibleChars(pendingTranscript),
    };

    await writeJsonFile(sidebarPath, sidebarState);
  } catch (_err) {
    // best-effort persistence
  }
}

ipcMain.handle("workspace:start-realtime-transcription", async (_, payload) => {
  const workspaceId = String(payload?.workspaceId || "").trim();
  const chunkDurationMs = [50, 100, 150].includes(payload?.chunkDurationMs)
    ? payload.chunkDurationMs
    : 100;
  const sampleRate = Number(payload?.sampleRate) || 16000;

  if (!workspaceId) {
    return { ok: false, message: "缺少工作区标识。" };
  }

  const existingSession = realtimeSessions.get(workspaceId);
  if (existingSession?.ws) {
    return { ok: false, message: "当前工作区已有正在执行的实时转写。" };
  }

  const cacheRootPath = getWorkspaceCacheRoot();
  const { workspace } = await getIndexedWorkspace(cacheRootPath, workspaceId);
  if (!workspace) {
    return { ok: false, message: "没有找到对应的工作区。" };
  }

  const session = createRealtimeSession(workspaceId, chunkDurationMs, sampleRate);
  realtimeSessions.set(workspaceId, session);
  void connectDeepgramWebSocket(session);

  const startedAtUtc = new Date().toISOString();
  const status = setWorkspacePipelineStatus(workspaceId, {
    runState: "running",
    processingStatus: "connecting",
    startedAtUtc,
    message: "正在启动实时流式转写...",
  });

  return { ok: true, status };
});

ipcMain.handle("workspace:push-realtime-audio-chunk", async (_, payload) => {
  const workspaceId = String(payload?.workspaceId || "").trim();
  const session = realtimeSessions.get(workspaceId);
  if (!session) return;

  let audioBuffer;
  if (payload?.audio instanceof Uint8Array) {
    audioBuffer = Buffer.from(payload.audio);
  } else if (payload?.audio?.data) {
    audioBuffer = Buffer.from(payload.audio.data);
  } else {
    return;
  }

  if (session.ws && session.ws.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
    session.ws.send(audioBuffer);
  } else {
    session.pendingChunks.push(audioBuffer);
    if (session.pendingChunks.length > 50) {
      session.pendingChunks.shift();
    }
  }
});

ipcMain.handle("workspace:stop-realtime-transcription", async (_, workspaceId) => {
  const nextWorkspaceId = String(workspaceId || "").trim();
  if (!nextWorkspaceId) {
    return { ok: false, message: "缺少工作区标识。" };
  }

  const session = realtimeSessions.get(nextWorkspaceId);
  if (session) {
    session.shouldReconnect = false;
    if (session.ws) {
      try {
        session.ws.send(JSON.stringify({ type: "CloseStream" }));
      } catch {}
      try {
        session.ws.close();
      } catch {}
    }
    realtimeSessions.delete(nextWorkspaceId);
  }

  const cacheRootPath = getWorkspaceCacheRoot();
  const { workspace } = await getIndexedWorkspace(cacheRootPath, nextWorkspaceId);
  if (workspace) {
    await persistWorkspaceRecord(
      cacheRootPath,
      createWorkspaceRecord({ ...workspace, status: "paused", updatedAtUtc: new Date().toISOString() }),
    );
  }

  const status = setWorkspacePipelineStatus(nextWorkspaceId, {
    runState: "idle",
    processingStatus: "paused",
    message: "实时转写已停止。",
    finishedAtUtc: new Date().toISOString(),
  });

  return { ok: true, status };
});

  await createMainWindow();

```
    setExpandedSummaryId((currentId) => (currentId === summaryId ? "" : summaryId));
  }

  // ── Realtime streaming STT: mic capture via ScriptProcessorNode ──
  // Audio is captured continuously, converted to PCM16, and sent as
  // small chunks (default 100ms) via IPC to main process, which relays
  // to Deepgram's WebSocket. This is true streaming, not record-then-upload.

  const stopMicCapture = useCallback(() => {
    if (scriptNodeRef.current) {
      scriptNodeRef.current.disconnect();
      scriptNodeRef.current = null;
    }
    if (mediaStreamRef.current) {
      for (const track of mediaStreamRef.current.getTracks()) {
        track.stop();
      }
      mediaStreamRef.current = null;
    }
    if (audioContextRef.current) {
      void audioContextRef.current.close();
      audioContextRef.current = null;
    }
  }, []);

  const startMicCapture = useCallback(
    async (workspaceId: string, chunkDurationMs: number = 100) => {
      const bridge = window.desktopBridge;
      if (!bridge?.pushRealtimeAudioChunk) return;

      let stream: MediaStream;
      try {
        stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({
          audio: {
            channelCount: 1,
            sampleRate: 16000,
            echoCancellation: true,
            noiseSuppression: true,
          },
        });
      } catch (err) {
        setRealtimeError(
          err instanceof DOMException && err.name === "NotAllowedError"
            ? "麦克风权限被拒绝，请在系统设置中允许此应用使用麦克风。"
            : `麦克风访问失败: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)}`,
        );
        setRealtimeStatus("error");
        return;
      }

      mediaStreamRef.current = stream;
      const sampleRate = 16000;
      const ctx = new AudioContext({ sampleRate });
      audioContextRef.current = ctx;
      const source = ctx.createMediaStreamSource(stream);

      // ScriptProcessorNode: bufferSize chosen to approximate chunkDurationMs
      const bufferSize = Math.pow(
        2,
        Math.max(
          8,
          Math.ceil(Math.log2((sampleRate * chunkDurationMs) / 1000)),
        ),
      );
      const processor = ctx.createScriptProcessor(bufferSize, 1, 1);
      scriptNodeRef.current = processor;

      processor.onaudioprocess = (e: AudioProcessingEvent) => {
        const float32 = e.inputBuffer.getChannelData(0);

        // Compute RMS for basic VAD
        let sumSq = 0;
        for (let i = 0; i < float32.length; i++) {
          sumSq += float32[i] * float32[i];
        }
        const rms = Math.sqrt(sumSq / float32.length);
        const hasSpeech = rms > 0.01;

        // Convert Float32 to PCM16 (Linear16)
        const pcm16 = new Int16Array(float32.length);
        for (let i = 0; i < float32.length; i++) {
          const s = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, float32[i]));
          pcm16[i] = s < 0 ? s * 0x8000 : s * 0x7fff;
        }

        bridge.pushRealtimeAudioChunk({
          workspaceId,
          audio: new Uint8Array(pcm16.buffer),
          sampleRate,
          chunkDurationMs: chunkDurationMs as 50 | 100 | 150,
          rms,
          hasSpeech,
        });
      };

      source.connect(processor);
      processor.connect(ctx.destination);
    },
    [],
  );

  // Listen for realtime transcription events from main process
  useEffect(() => {
    const bridge = window.desktopBridge;
    if (!bridge?.onRealtimeTranscriptionEvent) return;

    const unsubscribe = bridge.onRealtimeTranscriptionEvent(
      (event: RealtimeTranscriptionEvent) => {
        if (event.workspaceId !== activeWorkspaceId) return;

        switch (event.type) {
          case "partial":
            setRealtimePartialText(event.partialText || "");
            setRealtimeStatus("streaming");
            break;
          case "final":
            if (event.finalSegment) {
              setRealtimeSegments((prev) => [...prev, event.finalSegment!]);
            }
            setRealtimeFinalText(event.finalTranscriptText || "");
            setRealtimePartialText("");
            setRealtimeStatus("streaming");
            break;
          case "status":
            setRealtimeStatus(event.status);
            break;
          case "error":
            setRealtimeError(event.lastError || "实时转写出错。");
            setRealtimeStatus("error");
            break;
          case "stopped":
            setRealtimeStatus("idle");
            setIsRealtimeActive(false);
            break;
        }
      },
    );

    return unsubscribe;
  }, [activeWorkspaceId]);

  async function handleStartPipeline(): Promise<void> {
    if (!activeWorkspace) {
      setWorkspaceErrorMessage("请先选择一个工作区再开始转录。");
      return;
    }

    if (isRealtimeActive || isStartingPipeline) {
      return;
    }

    const bridge = window.desktopBridge;
    const useRealtime = !!bridge?.startRealtimeTranscription;

    setIsStartingPipeline(true);
    setWorkspaceErrorMessage("");
    setRealtimeError("");

    try {
      if (useRealtime) {
        setRealtimePartialText("");
        setRealtimeFinalText("");
        setRealtimeSegments([]);
        setRealtimeStatus("connecting");
        setIsRealtimeActive(true);

        const result = await bridge.startRealtimeTranscription({
          workspaceId: activeWorkspace.workspaceId,
          chunkDurationMs: 100,
        });

        if (!result.ok) {
          setWorkspaceErrorMessage(result.message || "启动实时转写失败。");
          setIsRealtimeActive(false);
          setRealtimeStatus("error");
          return;
        }

        if (result.status) {
          setPipelineStatus(result.status);
        }

        await startMicCapture(activeWorkspace.workspaceId, 100);
      } else {
        const nextStatus = await startNormalModeWorkspacePipeline({
          workspaceId: activeWorkspace.workspaceId,
        });
        setPipelineStatus(nextStatus);
        setRecentWorkspaces((previousWorkspaces) =>
          previousWorkspaces.map((workspace) =>
            workspace.workspaceId === activeWorkspace.workspaceId
              ? { ...workspace, status: nextStatus.processingStatus }
              : workspace,
          ),
        );
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("启动工作区转录失败", error);
      setWorkspaceErrorMessage(
        error instanceof Error ? error.message : "启动工作区转录失败。",
      );
      setIsRealtimeActive(false);
      setRealtimeStatus("error");
    } finally {
      setIsStartingPipeline(false);
    }

## Assistant

Typecheck and build both pass.所有任务完成。以下是改动总结：

## 核心改动

**架构**：从"录制 N 秒 → 停止 → 上传 → 再录制"的轮询方案，改为**真正的流式 STT**：
- 前端通过 `ScriptProcessorNode` 持续采集麦克风音频，转成 PCM16 小块（默认 100ms/块）
- 经 IPC 发送到 Electron 主进程
- 主进程维护一条 WebSocket 到 Deepgram `nova-2` 流式转写
- partial/final 结果实时推回前端

**修改的文件**：
1. **`electron/preload.cjs`** — 新增 4 个 IPC 方法（start/push/stop/onEvent）
2. **`src/vite-env.d.ts`** — DesktopBridge 接口添加对应类型声明
3. **`electron/main.js`** — 引入 `ws`，实现 Deepgram WebSocket 会话管理（连接、重连、partial/final 解析、sidebar 持久化）
4. **`src/App.tsx`** — 麦克风采集（ScriptProcessor + PCM16 转换 + RMS VAD）、事件监听、启动/暂停逻辑
5. **`src/components/SummarySidebar.tsx`** — 新增实时转录面板（状态指示灯、final segments、partial 文字）
6. **`src/styles.css`** — 实时转录面板样式

**下一步**：`npm run dev:desktop` 启动 Electron 即可测试。需确保 Deepgram API Key 已在设置中配置。

## Plan

- [x] Add realtime transcription IPC methods to preload.cjs
- [x] Add new methods to DesktopBridge interface in vite-env.d.ts
- [x] Implement Deepgram WebSocket streaming + IPC handlers in electron/main.js
- [x] Implement frontend AudioWorklet microphone capture + VAD in App.tsx
- [x] Update SummarySidebar to display partial/final transcript
- [x] Typecheck and build verification

基于Arduino的六足机器人控制系统设计——毕业设计答辩演讲稿

尊敬的各位老师、同学们：
大家好！我是应用技术学院自动化专业的杨晨，今天我答辩的题目是基于Arduino的六足机器人控制系统设计。

在人工智能与机器人技术快速发展的今天，六足机器人凭借稳定性强、地形适应性好、越障能力突出的优势，在救援探测、军事侦察、复杂环境作业等领域具有广阔应用前景。本次设计以Arduino为核心控制平台，旨在实现一套集语音识别、目标跟踪、自主避障于一体的低成本、模块化六足机器人控制系统。

首先，在系统设计上，我采用Arduino UNO R3作为主控，搭配18路总线舵机构成六足仿生结构，每只足3个自由度，整体采用三角步态保证运动稳定。硬件部分选用超声波传感器、红外传感器、声音传感器，分别实现距离检测、避障判断与语音触发，配合舵机控制板完成动作执行，整体结构紧凑、扩展性强。

软件设计基于Arduino IDE开发，采用C语言编程。我完成了三大核心功能模块：第一，语音识别模块，通过声音传感器触发，可识别预设关键词，实现诗词朗诵、互动问答、动作切换等人机交互；第二，目标跟踪模块，利用云台超声波传感器扫描前方扇形区域，根据距离自动实现前进、后退、左右转向跟随；第三，自主避障模块，通过双侧红外传感器检测障碍，控制机器人左移、右移或后退绕行，提升复杂环境适应能力。

在实验测试中，系统整体运行稳定。语音识别响应灵敏，目标跟踪跟随流畅，自主避障判断准确，能够在无人工干预下完成“语音唤醒—目标跟踪—自主避障”的完整任务流程，达到了预期设计目标。同时，本设计具有成本低、模块化、易调试的特点，适合教学与小型科研场景使用。

当然，设计也存在不足：受传感器精度与结构限制，远距离跟踪与狭窄空间避障效果仍有提升空间。未来可加入视觉传感器或激光雷达，实现三维环境感知与目标锁定，进一步提升机器人的智能化水平。

最后，衷心感谢殷利平教授在毕业设计全过程中的悉心指导，也感谢各位老师的聆听。以上是我的毕业设计汇报，恳请各位老师批评指正，谢谢大家！

## Assistant

这是一个很好的测试案例。转录结果和原文差距确实很大，我来分析根本原因。

## 问题分析

### 1. Deepgram 模型选择不当
当前用的是 `nova-2` + `language: "zh"`。`nova-2` 对中文的支持远不如专门的中文模型，尤其是：
- 学术/专业术语（"Arduino"、"舵机"、"超声波传感器"）几乎全部丢失
- 长句结构被打碎成无意义片段
- 大量同音替换（"答辩" → "答电"、"避障" → 不识别）

### 2. 音频质量问题
- 毕业答辩场景通常有回声、距离麦克风远、环境噪音
- 100ms chunk 太小，Deepgram 难以获得足够上下文做语义纠错

### 3. 缺少领域词汇引导
- 没有传 `keywords` 参数来 boost 专业术语的识别概率
- 没有开启 `diarize`、`punctuate` 等辅助功能

### 4. VAD 问题
- 出现大量重复片段（"不要问他"重复 10 次），说明静音判断或 utterance 边界有问题

## 解决方案

我来修改 Deepgram 的连接参数，提升中文识别精度。

function buildDeepgramStreamingUrl(sampleRate, keywords) {
  const params = new URLSearchParams({
    model: "nova-3",
    language: "zh",
    smart_format: "true",
    punctuate: "true",
    interim_results: "true",
    utterance_end_ms: "2000",
    vad_events: "true",
    endpointing: "400",
    encoding: "linear16",
    sample_rate: String(sampleRate),
    channels: "1",
  });
  if (Array.isArray(keywords) && keywords.length > 0) {
    for (const kw of keywords) {
      params.append("keywords", kw);
    }
  }
  return `wss://api.deepgram.com/v1/listen?${params.toString()}`;
}

```

ipcMain.handle("workspace:start-realtime-transcription", async (_, payload) => {
  const workspaceId = String(payload?.workspaceId || "").trim();
  const chunkDurationMs = [50, 100, 150, 200, 250, 300].includes(payload?.chunkDurationMs)
    ? payload.chunkDurationMs
    : 200;
  const sampleRate = Number(payload?.sampleRate) || 16000;
  const keywords = Array.isArray(payload?.keywords) ? payload.keywords : [];

  if (!workspaceId) {
    return { ok: false, message: "缺少工作区标识。" };

## Assistant

全部通过。以下是精度问题的分析和已做的修改总结：

## 精度差的原因

| 问题 | 影响 |
|------|------|
| **模型错误**：用的 `nova-2` 对中文支持弱 | 大量同音错字、专业术语完全丢失 |
| **chunk 太小**（100ms）| Deepgram 缺少上下文做纠错，碎片化严重 |
| **utterance_end_ms 太短**（1500ms）| 说话稍有停顿就被切断，产生断句 |
| **没开 punctuate** | 无标点辅助，语义边界模糊 |
| **没有 keywords boost** | "Arduino"、"舵机"、"避障"等术语无法引导识别 |
| **没有 endpointing** | 没有控制 Deepgram 何时判定一个语句结束 |

## 已做的修改

1. **模型升级**：`nova-2` → `nova-3`（Deepgram 最新中文模型，精度大幅提升）
2. **chunk 加大**：默认 100ms → 200ms，给模型更多上下文
3. **utterance_end_ms 加长**：1500ms → 2000ms，减少误切断
4. **新增 endpointing: 400ms**：控制语句结束判定，避免碎片
5. **开启 punctuate**：自动标点，改善可读性
6. **支持 keywords 参数**：可传入领域术语（如 `["Arduino", "舵机", "避障", "超声波传感器"]`）来 boost 识别

## 进一步提升建议

- **使用前传入 keywords**：针对每次答辩的主题词预设关键词列表，会显著提升专业术语的识别率
- **如果 Deepgram 精度仍不够**：可考虑换用阿里云 ASR 或讯飞的流式识别，中文支持更好
- **麦克风质量**：尽量使用近场麦克风（如领夹麦），远场采集是精度下降的主因

## Assistant

构建通过。修改内容：

1. **添加 KeepAlive 心跳**（每 8 秒）— Deepgram 在没收到音频或 KeepAlive 约 10 秒后会主动断开连接，这是内容只有前几秒的根本原因
2. **AudioContext 挂起恢复** — 浏览器可能创建 AudioContext 时就是 suspended 状态，导致音频采集中断
3. **在 close/stop 时清理 KeepAlive 定时器**，避免内存泄漏

重启 `npm run dev:desktop` 测试即可。

```
import type {
  SummaryCard,
  SummaryEmptyState,
  TranscriptSegment,
} from "../types";

interface SummarySidebarProps {
  summaries: ReadonlyArray<SummaryCard>;
  expandedSummaryId: string;
  emptyState?: SummaryEmptyState;
  onToggleSummary: (summaryId: string) => void;
  realtimePartialText?: string;
  realtimeSegments?: ReadonlyArray<TranscriptSegment>;
  realtimeStatus?: string;
  realtimeError?: string;
}

export default function SummarySidebar({
  summaries,
  expandedSummaryId,
  emptyState,
  onToggleSummary,
  realtimePartialText,
  realtimeSegments,
  realtimeStatus,
  realtimeError,
}: SummarySidebarProps) {
  const hasRealtimeContent =
    realtimeStatus === "streaming" ||
    realtimeStatus === "connecting" ||
    realtimeStatus === "listening" ||
    (realtimeSegments && realtimeSegments.length > 0) ||
    realtimePartialText;

  const realtimePanel = hasRealtimeContent ? (
    <div className="realtime-transcript-panel">
      <div className="realtime-transcript-header">
        <span>实时转录</span>
        <span
          className={`realtime-status-dot ${realtimeStatus === "streaming" ? "active" : realtimeStatus === "error" ? "error" : ""}`}
        />
        <span className="realtime-status-label">
          {realtimeStatus === "streaming"
            ? "转录中"
            : realtimeStatus === "connecting"
              ? "连接中"
              : realtimeStatus === "listening"
                ? "等待语音"
                : realtimeStatus === "error"
                  ? "出错"
                  : "就绪"}
        </span>
      </div>
      {realtimeError ? (
        <div className="realtime-error">{realtimeError}</div>
      ) : null}
      <div className="realtime-transcript-body">
        {realtimeSegments?.map((seg) => (
          <p key={seg.id} className="realtime-final-segment">
            {seg.text}
          </p>
        ))}
        {realtimePartialText ? (
          <p className="realtime-partial-text">{realtimePartialText}</p>
        ) : null}
      </div>
    </div>
  ) : null;

  if (!summaries.length) {
    return (
      <div className="summary-panel">
        {realtimePanel}
        <div className="summary-empty">
          <div className="summary-empty-title">
            {emptyState?.title || "等待转录与总结结果"}
          </div>
          <div className="summary-empty-copy">
            {emptyState?.copy || "上传文件后，后端生成的分段总结会显示在这里。"}
          </div>
          {emptyState?.transcriptPreview ? (
            <div className="summary-empty-transcript">
              <div className="summary-empty-transcript-label">本次转录结果</div>
              <p>{emptyState.transcriptPreview}</p>
            </div>
          ) : null}
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="summary-panel">
      {realtimePanel}
      <div className="summary-list">
        {summaries.map((item, index) => {
          const isExpanded = expandedSummaryId === item.id;

          return (
            <article
              className={`summary-item ${isExpanded ? "expanded" : ""}`}
              key={item.id}
            >
              <button
                className="summary-trigger"
                type="button"
                onClick={() => onToggleSummary(item.id)}
              >
                <div className="summary-trigger-top">
                  <span className="summary-index">
                    {String(index + 1).padStart(2, "0")}
                  </span>
                  <span className="summary-chevron">{isExpanded ? "−" : "+"}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="summary-copy">{item.summary}</div>
              </button>

              <div
                className={`summary-transcript-wrap ${isExpanded ? "expanded" : ""}`}
              >
                <div className="summary-transcript">
                  <div className="summary-transcript-label">转录内容</div>
                  <p>{item.transcript}</p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </article>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

```

## Assistant

构建通过。改动总结：

**精度提升：**
- `numerals: true` — 数字识别更准确（如"18路"、"16kHz"）
- `profanity_filter: false` — 避免误过滤专业术语
- `filler_words: false` — 去掉"嗯啊"等填充词，结果更干净
- `endpointing: 400 → 500` — 给模型更多上下文再断句，减少碎片

**调试窗口已删除：**
- 移除了 `SummarySidebar` 中的 debugPanel 及相关 props/CSS
- CSS 中 `.summary-debug*` 样式全部清理

"""
Local streaming ASR server using faster-whisper + Silero VAD.

Architecture:
- Starts a WebSocket server on a random available port.
- Prints the port number to stdout so the Electron main process can connect.
- Receives PCM16 mono 16kHz audio chunks from the client.
- Uses Silero VAD to detect speech boundaries.
- Runs faster-whisper on completed speech segments for high-accuracy transcription.
- Sends partial (during speech) and final (after speech ends) results back.
- Message format is compatible with the existing Deepgram event handler in main.js.
"""

import argparse
import asyncio
import json
import io
import struct
import sys
import time
import uuid
import logging
import wave
import numpy as np

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="[asr] %(message)s")
log = logging.getLogger("asr_server")

try:
    import websockets
except ImportError:
    log.error("websockets 未安装，请运行: pip install websockets")
    sys.exit(1)

try:
    from faster_whisper import WhisperModel
except ImportError:
    log.error("faster-whisper 未安装，请运行: pip install faster-whisper")
    sys.exit(1)

try:
    import torch
except ImportError:
    torch = None

# ── Silero VAD ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class SileroVAD:
    def __init__(self, threshold=0.4, min_speech_ms=250, min_silence_ms=800, sample_rate=16000):
        self.threshold = threshold
        self.min_speech_samples = int(min_speech_ms * sample_rate / 1000)
        self.min_silence_samples = int(min_silence_ms * sample_rate / 1000)
        self.sample_rate = sample_rate
        self.model = None
        self._load_model()

    def _load_model(self):
        if torch is None:
            log.warning("torch 未安装，VAD 将使用 RMS 能量检测替代 Silero")
            return
        try:
            self.model, _ = torch.hub.load(
                repo_or_dir="snakers4/silero-vad",
                model="silero_vad",
                force_reload=False,
                trust_repo=True,
            )
            log.info("Silero VAD 模型已加载")
        except Exception as e:
            log.warning(f"Silero VAD 加载失败，将使用 RMS 能量检测: {e}")
            self.model = None

    def get_speech_prob(self, audio_chunk_int16):
        float32 = audio_chunk_int16.astype(np.float32) / 32768.0
        if self.model is not None and torch is not None:
            tensor = torch.from_numpy(float32)
            prob = self.model(tensor, self.sample_rate).item()
            return prob
        else:
            rms = np.sqrt(np.mean(float32 ** 2))
            return min(1.0, rms / 0.02)


class SpeechSegmenter:
    """Accumulates audio and uses VAD to detect speech segments."""

    def __init__(self, vad, sample_rate=16000):
        self.vad = vad
        self.sample_rate = sample_rate
        self.buffer = bytearray()
        self.is_speaking = False
        self.speech_start_bytes = 0
        self.silence_bytes = 0
        self.min_speech_bytes = vad.min_speech_samples * 2
        self.min_silence_bytes = vad.min_silence_samples * 2
        # Max segment length: 30 seconds (Whisper's context window)
        self.max_segment_bytes = 30 * sample_rate * 2

    def feed(self, pcm16_bytes):
        """Feed raw PCM16 bytes. Returns list of completed speech segments (bytes)."""
        self.buffer.extend(pcm16_bytes)
        completed = []

        chunk_size = 512 * 2  # 512 samples = 32ms at 16kHz
        while len(self.buffer) - self.speech_start_bytes >= chunk_size:
            offset = len(self.buffer) - chunk_size
            chunk = np.frombuffer(
                bytes(self.buffer[offset:offset + chunk_size]),
                dtype=np.int16,
            )
            prob = self.vad.get_speech_prob(chunk)

            if prob >= self.vad.threshold:
                if not self.is_speaking:
                    self.is_speaking = True
                    # Start a bit before current position for context
                    ctx = min(self.sample_rate * 2, offset)  # up to 1s context
                    self.speech_start_bytes = max(0, offset - ctx)
                self.silence_bytes = 0
            else:
                if self.is_speaking:
                    self.silence_bytes += chunk_size

            # Check if speech segment is complete (silence after speech)
            if self.is_speaking and self.silence_bytes >= self.min_silence_bytes:
                speech_end = len(self.buffer) - self.silence_bytes
                seg_bytes = bytes(self.buffer[self.speech_start_bytes:speech_end])
                if len(seg_bytes) >= self.min_speech_bytes:
                    completed.append(seg_bytes)
                self.buffer = bytearray(self.buffer[speech_end:])
                self.speech_start_bytes = 0
                self.silence_bytes = 0
                self.is_speaking = False

            # Force segment if too long
            if self.is_speaking:
                current_len = len(self.buffer) - self.speech_start_bytes
                if current_len >= self.max_segment_bytes:
                    seg_bytes = bytes(self.buffer[self.speech_start_bytes:len(self.buffer)])
                    completed.append(seg_bytes)
                    self.buffer = bytearray()
                    self.speech_start_bytes = 0
                    self.silence_bytes = 0
                    self.is_speaking = False

            break  # Only process one chunk per feed to keep latency low

        return completed

    def get_pending_audio(self):
        """Get currently accumulated speech audio (for partial results)."""
        if self.is_speaking and len(self.buffer) > self.speech_start_bytes + self.min_speech_bytes:
            return bytes(self.buffer[self.speech_start_bytes:])
        return None


# ── Whisper transcription ───────────────────────────────────────────

class WhisperTranscriber:
    def __init__(self, model_size="large-v3", device="auto", compute_type="auto"):
        if device == "auto":
            if torch is not None and torch.cuda.is_available():
                device = "cuda"
                if compute_type == "auto":
                    compute_type = "float16"
            elif torch is not None and hasattr(torch.backends, "mps") and torch.backends.mps.is_available():
                device = "cpu"  # faster-whisper doesn't support MPS directly
                if compute_type == "auto":
                    compute_type = "int8"
            else:
                device = "cpu"
                if compute_type == "auto":
                    compute_type = "int8"

        log.info(f"加载 Whisper 模型: {model_size} (device={device}, compute={compute_type})")
        self.model = WhisperModel(model_size, device=device, compute_type=compute_type)
        log.info("Whisper 模型加载完成")

    def transcribe_pcm16(self, pcm16_bytes, sample_rate=16000):
        """Transcribe raw PCM16 audio bytes. Returns text string."""
        audio_int16 = np.frombuffer(pcm16_bytes, dtype=np.int16)
        audio_float32 = audio_int16.astype(np.float32) / 32768.0

        segments, info = self.model.transcribe(
            audio_float32,
            language="zh",
            beam_size=5,
            vad_filter=True,
            vad_parameters=dict(
                min_silence_duration_ms=500,
                speech_pad_ms=200,
            ),
        )

        texts = []
        for seg in segments:
            text = seg.text.strip()
            if text:
                texts.append(text)

        return "".join(texts)


# ── WebSocket server ────────────────────────────────────────────────

whisper_model = None
vad = None


async def handle_client(websocket):
    global whisper_model, vad

    segmenter = SpeechSegmenter(vad)
    partial_timer = 0
    PARTIAL_INTERVAL = 2.0  # seconds between partial transcriptions

    log.info("客户端已连接")

    try:
        async for message in websocket:
            if isinstance(message, str):
                try:
                    msg = json.loads(message)
                    if msg.get("type") == "CloseStream":
                        log.info("收到 CloseStream，关闭连接")
                        break
                    if msg.get("type") == "KeepAlive":
                        continue
                except json.JSONDecodeError:
                    pass
                continue

            # Binary message = PCM16 audio data
            completed_segments = segmenter.feed(message)

            # Process completed speech segments
            for seg_bytes in completed_segments:
                text = whisper_model.transcribe_pcm16(seg_bytes)
                if text.strip():
                    result = {
                        "type": "Results",
                        "is_final": True,
                        "channel": {
                            "alternatives": [{"transcript": text}]
                        },
                    }
                    await websocket.send(json.dumps(result))

            # Periodic partial results while speaking
            now = time.time()
            if now - partial_timer >= PARTIAL_INTERVAL:
                partial_timer = now
                pending = segmenter.get_pending_audio()
                if pending and len(pending) > segmenter.min_speech_bytes:
                    text = whisper_model.transcribe_pcm16(pending)
                    if text.strip():
                        result = {
                            "type": "Results",
                            "is_final": False,
                            "channel": {
                                "alternatives": [{"transcript": text}]
                            },
                        }
                        await websocket.send(json.dumps(result))

    except websockets.exceptions.ConnectionClosed:
        log.info("客户端断开连接")
    except Exception as e:
        log.error(f"处理音频时出错: {e}")


async def start_server(host, port, model_size, device, compute_type):
    global whisper_model, vad

    log.info("正在初始化 VAD...")
    vad = SileroVAD(threshold=0.4, min_speech_ms=250, min_silence_ms=800)

    log.info("正在加载 Whisper 模型...")
    whisper_model = WhisperTranscriber(
        model_size=model_size,
        device=device,
        compute_type=compute_type,
    )

    server = await websockets.serve(handle_client, host, port)
    actual_port = server.sockets[0].getsockname()[1]

    # Print port for Electron to read
    print(f"ASR_SERVER_PORT={actual_port}", flush=True)
    log.info(f"ASR 服务已启动: ws://{host}:{actual_port}")

    await server.wait_closed()


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Local ASR WebSocket server")
    parser.add_argument("--host", default="127.0.0.1")
    parser.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=0, help="0 = random available port")
    parser.add_argument("--model", default="large-v3",
                        help="Whisper model size: tiny, base, small, medium, large-v3")
    parser.add_argument("--device", default="auto", help="cpu, cuda, or auto")
    parser.add_argument("--compute-type", default="auto", help="int8, float16, or auto")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    asyncio.run(start_server(args.host, args.port, args.model, args.device, args.compute_type))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```

  };

// ── Realtime streaming STT via local faster-whisper ASR server ──────
// Architecture: Electron spawns a local Python WebSocket server running
// faster-whisper + Silero VAD. Renderer captures mic audio via
// ScriptProcessorNode, sends PCM16 chunks through IPC. Main process
// relays audio to the local ASR server. Partial/final results are
// pushed back to renderer via webContents.send().

const realtimeSessions = new Map();
let globalAsrProcess = null;
let globalAsrPort = null;
let globalAsrReady = null; // Promise that resolves when ASR server is ready
const ASR_SERVER_SCRIPT = path.join(projectRoot, "lite_synphonia", "asr_server.py");
const ASR_MODEL_SIZE = "large-v3";

function emitRealtimeEvent(workspaceId, event) {
  for (const win of BrowserWindow.getAllWindows()) {
```


Diff: /Users/wangwenbin/projects/比赛项目前端/electron/main.js
```
  }
}

async function ensureAsrServer() {
  if (globalAsrProcess && globalAsrPort) {
    return globalAsrPort;
  }

  if (globalAsrReady) {
    return globalAsrReady;
  }

  globalAsrReady = new Promise(async (resolve, reject) => {
    try {
      const pythonExecutable = await getLiteSynphoniaPythonExecutable();

      const proc = spawn(pythonExecutable, [
        ASR_SERVER_SCRIPT,
        "--model", ASR_MODEL_SIZE,
        "--port", "0",
      ], {
        cwd: projectRoot,
        stdio: ["ignore", "pipe", "pipe"],
      });

      globalAsrProcess = proc;
      let resolved = false;

      proc.stdout.on("data", (chunk) => {
        const text = chunk.toString("utf8");
        for (const line of text.split(/\r?\n/)) {
          const portMatch = line.match(/ASR_SERVER_PORT=(\d+)/);
          if (portMatch && !resolved) {
            resolved = true;
            globalAsrPort = Number(portMatch[1]);
            resolve(globalAsrPort);
          }
        }
      });

      proc.stderr.on("data", (chunk) => {
        const text = chunk.toString("utf8").trim();
        if (text) {
          console.log(`[asr-server] ${text}`);
        }
      });

      proc.on("error", (err) => {
        globalAsrProcess = null;
        globalAsrPort = null;
        globalAsrReady = null;
        if (!resolved) {
          resolved = true;
          reject(new Error(`ASR 服务启动失败: ${err.message}`));
        }
      });

      proc.on("close", (code) => {
        globalAsrProcess = null;
        globalAsrPort = null;
        globalAsrReady = null;
        if (!resolved) {
          resolved = true;
          reject(new Error(`ASR 服务退出 (code=${code})，请检查 Python 依赖是否安装。`));
        }
      });

      // Timeout after 120s (model loading can be slow on first run)
      setTimeout(() => {
        if (!resolved) {
          resolved = true;
          reject(new Error("ASR 服务启动超时（120s），请检查 faster-whisper 和依赖是否安装。"));
        }
      }, 120000);
    } catch (err) {
      globalAsrReady = null;
      reject(err);
    }
  });

  return globalAsrReady;
}

function killAsrServer() {
  if (globalAsrProcess && !globalAsrProcess.killed) {
    try {
      globalAsrProcess.kill("SIGTERM");
    } catch {}
  }
  globalAsrProcess = null;
  globalAsrPort = null;
  globalAsrReady = null;
}

function createRealtimeSession(workspaceId, chunkDurationMs, sampleRate) {
  return {
    workspaceId,
    chunkDurationMs,
    sampleRate,
    ws: null,
    partialText: "",
    finalTranscriptText: "",
    finalSegments: [],
    reconnectAttempts: 0,
    maxReconnectAttempts: 3,
    shouldReconnect: true,
    pendingChunks: [],
    keepAliveTimer: null,
  };
}

async function connectLocalAsrWebSocket(session) {
  let port;
  try {
    port = await ensureAsrServer();
  } catch (err) {
    emitRealtimeEvent(session.workspaceId, {
      type: "error",
      status: "error",
      lastError: err.message,
    });
    return;
  }

    sidebarState.updatedAtUtc = new Date().toISOString();
    sidebarState.schemaVersion = "2.0";

    let pendingTranscript =
      (sidebarState.summaryWindow?.pendingTranscript || "") + segment.text;
    let generatedCount = Number(sidebarState.summaryWindow?.generatedCount) ||
      (Array.isArray(sidebarState.items) ? sidebarState.items.length : 0);

    // Sliding window: when pending text >= 200 chars, generate summary
    if (
      countVisibleChars(pendingTranscript) >= SUMMARY_WINDOW_TRIGGER_CHARS &&
      !realtimeSummaryLocks.get(workspace.workspaceId)
    ) {
      realtimeSummaryLocks.set(workspace.workspaceId, true);

      try {
        while (countVisibleChars(pendingTranscript) >= SUMMARY_WINDOW_TRIGGER_CHARS) {
          const transcriptWindow = takeFirstVisibleChars(
            pendingTranscript,
            SUMMARY_WINDOW_TRIGGER_CHARS,
          );

          let summaryResult;
          try {
            summaryResult = await runLiteSynphoniaSummaryWindow(transcriptWindow);
          } catch (err) {
            console.error("[summary-window] 生成总结失败:", err.message);
            break;
          }

          generatedCount += 1;
          const newItem = {
            id: `summary-window-${generatedCount}`,
            summary: summaryResult?.summary || "",
            transcript: transcriptWindow,
            transcriptSegmentIds: [],
            transcriptRange: { startTime: null, endTime: null },
          };

          if (!Array.isArray(sidebarState.items)) {
            sidebarState.items = [];
          }
          sidebarState.items.push(newItem);

          // Emit new summary item to frontend immediately
          emitRealtimeEvent(workspace.workspaceId, {
            type: "summary",
            status: "streaming",
            summaryItem: newItem,
          });

          // Slide window: keep last 50 chars
          const overflowTranscript = sliceAfterVisibleChars(
            pendingTranscript,
            SUMMARY_WINDOW_TRIGGER_CHARS,
          );
          const carryTranscript = takeLastVisibleChars(
            transcriptWindow,
            SUMMARY_WINDOW_OVERLAP_CHARS,
          );
          pendingTranscript = joinTextBlocks(carryTranscript, overflowTranscript);
        }
      } finally {
        realtimeSummaryLocks.delete(workspace.workspaceId);
      }
    }

    sidebarState.summaryWindow = {
      ...sidebarState.summaryWindow,
      triggerChars: SUMMARY_WINDOW_TRIGGER_CHARS,
      overlapChars: SUMMARY_WINDOW_OVERLAP_CHARS,
      pendingTranscript,
      pendingChars: countVisibleChars(pendingTranscript),
      generatedCount,

```
import type {
  SummaryCard,
  SummaryEmptyState,
} from "../types";

interface SummarySidebarProps {
  summaries: ReadonlyArray<SummaryCard>;
  expandedSummaryId: string;
  emptyState?: SummaryEmptyState;
  onToggleSummary: (summaryId: string) => void;
}

export default function SummarySidebar({
  summaries,
  expandedSummaryId,
  emptyState,
  onToggleSummary,
}: SummarySidebarProps) {
  if (!summaries.length) {
    return (
      <div className="summary-panel">
        <div className="summary-empty">
          <div className="summary-empty-title">
            {emptyState?.title || "等待转录与总结结果"}
          </div>
          <div className="summary-empty-copy">
            {emptyState?.copy || "上传文件后，后端生成的分段总结会显示在这里。"}
          </div>
          {emptyState?.transcriptPreview ? (
            <div className="summary-empty-transcript">
              <div className="summary-empty-transcript-label">本次转录结果</div>
              <p>{emptyState.transcriptPreview}</p>
            </div>
          ) : null}
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="summary-panel">
      <div className="summary-list">
        {summaries.map((item, index) => {
          const isExpanded = expandedSummaryId === item.id;

          return (
            <article
              className={`summary-item ${isExpanded ? "expanded" : ""}`}
              key={item.id}
            >
              <button
                className="summary-trigger"
                type="button"
                onClick={() => onToggleSummary(item.id)}
              >
                <div className="summary-trigger-top">
                  <span className="summary-index">
                    {String(index + 1).padStart(2, "0")}
                  </span>
                  <span className="summary-chevron">{isExpanded ? "−" : "+"}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="summary-copy">{item.summary}</div>
              </button>

              <div
                className={`summary-transcript-wrap ${isExpanded ? "expanded" : ""}`}
              >
                <div className="summary-transcript">
                  <div className="summary-transcript-label">转录内容</div>
                  <p>{item.transcript}</p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </article>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

```

## Assistant

构建通过。改动总结：

**滑动窗口总结逻辑：**
- 转录文本每到 **200 字**自动触发 DeepSeek 生成总结
- 总结生成后保留最后 **50 字**继续滑动窗口
- 总结一生成就通过 IPC `summary` 事件实时推送到前端，立即显示为 Normal 模式的摘要卡片

**左边栏元素结构：**
- 每张摘要卡片 = 总结内容 + 可展开的转录依据（点击展开看原始转录文本）

**已移除：**
- 实时转录内容展示面板（partial/final text 不再显示）
- 相关 CSS 样式、组件 props、状态变量

32	        help="Registered summarization provider name.",
33	    )
34	    parser.add_argument(
35	        "--language",
36	        type=str,
37	        default="auto",
38	        choices=["auto", "zh", "en"],
39	        help="Prompt language selection.",
40	    )
41	    parser.add_argument(
42	        "--max-new-tokens",
43	        type=int,
44	        default=384,
45	        help="Upper bound for generated tokens.",
46	    )
47	    return parser
48	
49	
50	def _load_text(args: argparse.Namespace) -> str:
51	    inline_text = str(args.text or "").strip()
52	    if inline_text:
53	        return inline_text
54	
55	    input_file = str(args.input_file or "").strip()
56	    if not input_file:
57	        raise ValueError("Either --text or --input-file must be provided.")
58	
59	    return Path(input_file).read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
60	
61	
62	def _build_client(provider_name: str, max_new_tokens: int) -> ApiLLMClient:
63	    registry = get_registry()
64	    entry = registry.get(provider_name)
65	    api_config = ApiConfig(
66	        base_url=entry.base_url,
67	        model_id=entry.model_id or provider_name,
68	        api_key_env="",
69	        max_new_tokens=max_new_tokens,
70	        temperature=entry.temperature if entry.temperature >= 0 else 0.1,
71	        timeout_seconds=entry.timeout_seconds if entry.timeout_seconds > 0 else 60.0,
72	        max_retries=entry.max_retries if entry.max_retries > 0 else 3,
73	        provider_name=provider_name,
74	    )
75	    client = ApiLLMClient(api_config, provider_name=provider_name)
76	    client.ensure_ready()
77	    return client
78	
79	
80	def run_summary_window_command(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
81	    args = _build_parser().parse_args(argv)
82	
83	    try:
84	        text = _load_text(args)
85	        if not text:
86	            raise ValueError("Transcript window text is empty.")
87	
88	        client = _build_client(args.provider, args.max_new_tokens)
89	        prompt = build_summarization_prompt(text, language=args.language)
90	        raw_response = client.generate_summary(prompt)
91	        parsed = parse_model_response(raw_response)
92	
93	        if parsed.status in {"success", "success_recovered"}:
94	            payload = {
95	                "ok": True,
96	                "status": parsed.status,
97	                "keywords": parsed.keywords,
98	                "summary": parsed.summary,
99	                "raw_response": raw_response,
100	                "error_message": parsed.error_message,
101	                "provider": args.provider,
102	            }
103	        else:
104	            heuristic = summarize_text(text, keyword_limit=5)
105	            payload = {
106	                "ok": True,
107	                "status": "fallback_heuristic",
108	                "keywords": heuristic.keywords,
109	                "summary": heuristic.summary,
110	                "raw_response": raw_response,
111	                "error_message": parsed.error_message,
112	                "provider": args.provider,
113	            }
114	
115	        sys.stdout.write(json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False))
116	        sys.stdout.flush()
117	        return 0
118	    except (ApiCallError, ValueError, FileNotFoundError, KeyError) as exc:
119	        sys.stderr.write(str(exc))
120	        sys.stderr.flush()
121	        return 1
122	
123	
124	if __name__ == "__main__":
125	    raise SystemExit(run_summary_window_command())
126

80	def run_summary_window_command(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
81	    args = _build_parser().parse_args(argv)
82	
83	    try:
84	        text = _load_text(args)
85	        if not text:
86	            raise ValueError("Transcript window text is empty.")
87	
88	        client = _build_client(args.provider, args.max_new_tokens)
89	        prompt = build_summarization_prompt(text, language=args.language)
90	        raw_response = client.generate_summary(prompt)
91	        parsed = parse_model_response(raw_response)
92	
93	        if parsed.status in {"success", "success_recovered"}:
94	            payload = {
95	                "ok": True,
96	                "status": parsed.status,
97	                "keywords": parsed.keywords,
98	                "summary": parsed.summary,
99	                "raw_response": raw_response,
100	                "error_message": parsed.error_message,
101	                "provider": args.provider,
102	            }
103	        else:
104	            heuristic = summarize_text(text, keyword_limit=5)
105	            payload = {
106	                "ok": True,
107	                "status": "fallback_heuristic",
108	                "keywords": heuristic.keywords,
109	                "summary": heuristic.summary,
110	                "raw_response": raw_response,
111	                "error_message": parsed.error_message,
112	                "provider": args.provider,
113	            }
114	
115	        sys.stdout.write(json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False))
116	        sys.stdout.flush()
117	        return 0
118	    except (ApiCallError, ValueError, FileNotFoundError, KeyError) as exc:
119	        sys.stderr.write(str(exc))
120	        sys.stderr.flush()
121	        return 1
122	
123	
124	if __name__ == "__main__":
125	    raise SystemExit(run_summary_window_command())
126

    dedup_section = ""
    if previous_summary:
        dedup_section = (
            "## 上一段摘要（仅供去重参考，不要重复其内容）\n"
            f"{previous_summary}\n\n"

        "## 任务\n"
        "阅读下方【讲座片段】，严格输出以下 JSON，不得包含任何其他内容：\n"

        "  - 保留原文中的英文缩写（如 MSE、SSIM、FFT、LPF）；\n"

def _build_english_prompt(window_text: str, previous_summary: str = "") -> str:
    dedup_section = ""
    if previous_summary:
        dedup_section = (
            "## Previous Summary (for deduplication only — do NOT repeat its content)\n"
            f"{previous_summary}\n\n"
        )

    return (
        "You are a professional lecture-notes editor specialising in dense technical content.\n\n"
        "## Task\n"
        "Read the [Transcript Window] below and output exactly this JSON — nothing else:\n"
        '{"keywords":["kw1","kw2","kw3"],"summary":"Summary text."}\n\n'
        + dedup_section
        + "## Rules\n"
        "keywords (array, 3-5 items):\n"
        "  - High-signal domain terms, technical nouns, key action verbs.\n"
        "  - Preserve technical abbreviations exactly (MSE, FFT, SSIM, LPF).\n"

const realtimeSummaryLocks = new Map();
const realtimeIdleTimers = new Map();
const realtimeLastSummaries = new Map();

async function generateSummaryFromWindow(workspace, sidebarState, pendingTranscript, generatedCount, clearAfter) {
  const workspaceId = workspace.workspaceId;
  const previousSummary = realtimeLastSummaries.get(workspaceId) || "";

  if (clearAfter) {
    const transcriptWindow = pendingTranscript;
    if (!countVisibleChars(transcriptWindow)) return { pendingTranscript, generatedCount };

    let summaryResult;
    try {
      summaryResult = await runLiteSynphoniaSummaryWindow(transcriptWindow, previousSummary);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("[summary-window] 生成总结失败:", err.message);
      return { pendingTranscript, generatedCount };
    }

    generatedCount += 1;
    const newItem = {
      id: `summary-window-${generatedCount}`,
      summary: summaryResult?.summary || "",
      transcript: transcriptWindow,
      transcriptSegmentIds: [],
      transcriptRange: { startTime: null, endTime: null },
    };

    if (!Array.isArray(sidebarState.items)) sidebarState.items = [];
    sidebarState.items.push(newItem);
    realtimeLastSummaries.set(workspaceId, summaryResult?.summary || "");

    emitRealtimeEvent(workspaceId, {
      type: "summary",
      status: "streaming",
      summaryItem: newItem,
    });

    pendingTranscript = "";
    return { pendingTranscript, generatedCount };
  }

  while (countVisibleChars(pendingTranscript) >= SUMMARY_WINDOW_TRIGGER_CHARS) {
    const transcriptWindow = takeFirstVisibleChars(
      pendingTranscript,
      SUMMARY_WINDOW_TRIGGER_CHARS,
    );

    let summaryResult;
    try {
      summaryResult = await runLiteSynphoniaSummaryWindow(transcriptWindow, previousSummary);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("[summary-window] 生成总结失败:", err.message);
      break;
    }

    generatedCount += 1;
    const newItem = {
      id: `summary-window-${generatedCount}`,
      summary: summaryResult?.summary || "",
      transcript: transcriptWindow,
      transcriptSegmentIds: [],
      transcriptRange: { startTime: null, endTime: null },
    };

    if (!Array.isArray(sidebarState.items)) sidebarState.items = [];
    sidebarState.items.push(newItem);
    realtimeLastSummaries.set(workspaceId, summaryResult?.summary || "");

    emitRealtimeEvent(workspaceId, {
      type: "summary",
      status: "streaming",
      summaryItem: newItem,
    });

    const overflowTranscript = sliceAfterVisibleChars(
      pendingTranscript,
      SUMMARY_WINDOW_TRIGGER_CHARS,
    );
    const carryTranscript = takeLastVisibleChars(
      transcriptWindow,
      SUMMARY_WINDOW_OVERLAP_CHARS,
    );
    pendingTranscript = joinTextBlocks(carryTranscript, overflowTranscript);
  }

  return { pendingTranscript, generatedCount };
}

async function flushSummaryWindow(workspace) {
  const workspaceId = workspace.workspaceId;
  if (realtimeSummaryLocks.get(workspaceId)) return;

  const sidebarPath = workspace.artifacts?.sidebarStatePath;
  if (!sidebarPath) return;

  let sidebarState;
  try {
    sidebarState = JSON.parse(await fsSync.readFileSync(sidebarPath, "utf8"));
  } catch {
    return;
  }

  const pendingTranscript = String(sidebarState.summaryWindow?.pendingTranscript || "").trim();
  if (!countVisibleChars(pendingTranscript)) return;

  realtimeSummaryLocks.set(workspaceId, true);
  try {
    let generatedCount = Number(sidebarState.summaryWindow?.generatedCount) ||
      (Array.isArray(sidebarState.items) ? sidebarState.items.length : 0);

    const result = await generateSummaryFromWindow(
      workspace, sidebarState, pendingTranscript, generatedCount, true,
    );

    sidebarState.summaryWindow = {
      ...sidebarState.summaryWindow,
      triggerChars: SUMMARY_WINDOW_TRIGGER_CHARS,
      overlapChars: SUMMARY_WINDOW_OVERLAP_CHARS,
      pendingTranscript: result.pendingTranscript,
      pendingChars: countVisibleChars(result.pendingTranscript),
      generatedCount: result.generatedCount,
    };

    await writeJsonFile(sidebarPath, sidebarState);
  } finally {
    realtimeSummaryLocks.delete(workspaceId);
  }
}

## Assistant

All changes are working. Here's a summary of everything implemented:

1. **3s idle timeout** — `realtimeIdleTimers` map tracks per-workspace timers. Each new transcript segment resets the timer. On timeout, `flushSummaryWindow()` summarizes pending text and clears the window entirely.

2. **Stop-listening flush** — The `workspace:stop-realtime-transcription` handler calls `flushSummaryWindow()` before stopping, ensuring any remaining window content gets summarized.

3. **Previous summary deduplication ("回测")** — `realtimeLastSummaries` map tracks the last generated summary per workspace. It's passed to `runLiteSynphoniaSummaryWindow()` → Python `--previous-summary` arg → prompt instructs DeepSeek to avoid repeating prior content.

4. **Realtime transcript indicator** — Single-line display at top of left sidebar showing current partial text with a pulsing green dot. Only visible when realtime transcription is active.

5. **Preview button styling** — Improved with larger padding, better font size/weight, and smoother transitions.

6. **Bottom content cutoff fix** — Added `height: 100%` and `overflow: hidden` to `.main-layer` so the grid child properly constrains its content within the viewport.
